  "old_string": "### Export Skill (EX)\n\n**Command:** `@Ferris EX`\n\n**Purpose:** Validate package structure, generate context snippets, and inject managed sections into CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md/.cursorrules.\n\n**When to Use:** When a skill is ready for CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md integration. Also provides distribution instructions for `npx skills publish`.\n\n**Key Steps:** Load skill → Validate package → Generate snippet → Update context file (CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md/.cursorrules) → Token report → Summary\n\n**Agent:** Ferris (Delivery mode)\n\n---\n\n## Workflow Connections",
  "new_string": "### Export Skill (EX)\n\n**Command:** `@Ferris EX`\n\n**Purpose:** Validate package structure, generate context snippets, and inject managed sections into CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md/.cursorrules.\n\n**When to Use:** When a skill is ready for CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md integration. Also provides distribution instructions for `npx skills publish`.\n\n**Key Steps:** Load skill → Validate package → Generate snippet → Update context file (CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md/.cursorrules) → Token report → Summary\n\n**Agent:** Ferris (Delivery mode)\n\n---\n\n## Management Workflows\n\n### Rename Skill (RS)\n\n**Command:** `@Ferris RS`\n\n**Purpose:** Rename a skill across all its versions. Because the agentskills.io spec requires `name` to match parent directory name, this is a coordinated move across outer/inner directories, SKILL.md frontmatter, metadata.json, context snippets, provenance maps, the export manifest, and platform context files.\n\n**When to Use:** You need to change a skill's name — for example, graduating a `QS`-generated skill (named from the repo) to a formal name, or adding a suffix like `-community` to distinguish from an official skill.\n\n**Key Steps:** Select skill + new name → Transactional copy → Update all references → Rebuild context files → Delete old name (point of no return)\n\n**Safety:** Transactional — if any step fails before the final delete, the old skill remains intact. Warns if `source_authority: \"official\"` (rename is local-only; published registry skill won't change).\n\n**Agent:** Ferris (Management mode)\n\n---\n\n### Drop Skill (DS)\n\n**Command:** `@Ferris DS`\n\n**Purpose:** Drop a specific skill version or an entire skill. Soft drop (default) marks the version as deprecated in the manifest and keeps files on disk. Hard drop (`--purge`) also deletes the files.\n\n**When to Use:** Retire a deprecated version (e.g., drop `cognee 0.1.0` because it's obsolete), free disk space, or remove a skill you no longer need.\n\n**Key Steps:** Select skill → Select version(s) + mode → Update manifest → Rebuild context files → Delete files (if purge)\n\n**Safety:** Active version guard — cannot drop the currently active version when other non-deprecated versions exist (switch active first, or drop all). Soft drop is reversible by editing the manifest.\n\n**Agent:** Ferris (Management mode)\n\n---\n\n## Workflow Connections"
}
```

{

  "file_path": "/home/armel/Projects/OSS/bmad-module-skill-forge/docs/concepts.md",
  "old_string": "When the source updates, you can re-run `@Ferris US` (update-skill) to regenerate the skill for the new version while preserving any manual additions you've made.\n\n---\n\n## BMAD Module",
  "new_string": "When the source updates, you can re-run `@Ferris US` (update-skill) to regenerate the skill for the new version while preserving any manual additions you've made.\n\n---\n\n## Skill Management\n\nTwo workflows let you rename and retire skills without manually editing files:\n\n**Rename (`@Ferris RS`)** — Change a skill's name across all its versions. Transactional: copies to the new name, verifies every reference, then deletes the old name only after verification succeeds. If anything fails mid-rename, the old skill stays intact. Use it to graduate quick-skills to formal names, or to add a suffix like `-community`.\n\n**Drop (`@Ferris DS`)** — Retire a specific version (e.g., drop `cognee 0.1.0` because it's deprecated) or an entire skill. Two modes:\n- **Soft drop (default)** marks the version as deprecated in the manifest and keeps files on disk. It stops appearing in CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md/.cursorrules immediately but is reversible by editing the manifest.\n- **Hard drop (purge)** also deletes the files from disk. Irreversible.\n\nBoth operations automatically rebuild platform context files so your AI agents see the updated state on the next session.\n\n---\n\n## BMAD Module"
}
```

194	The brief's `doc_urls` field drives the doc_fetcher step. The agent uses whatever web fetching tool is available in its environment (Firecrawl, WebFetch, curl, etc.) to retrieve documentation as markdown and extract API information with T3 citations.
195	
196	---
197	
198	## Tips & Tricks
199	
200	### Progressive Capability
201	
202	Start with Quick mode (no setup required), upgrade to Forge (install ast-grep), then Forge+ (install cocoindex-code for semantic discovery), then Deep (install QMD). Each tier builds on the previous — you never lose capability.
203	
204	### Batch Operations
205	
206	Use `--batch` with `create-skill` to process multiple briefs at once. Progress is checkpointed — if interrupted, re-run `@Ferris CS --batch` and Ferris will resume automatically from where it left off.
207	
208	### Stack Skills + Individual Skills
209	
210	Stack skills focus on integration patterns. Individual skills focus on API surface. Use both together for maximum coverage.
211	
212	### The Loop
213	
214	After each sprint's refactor, run `@Ferris US` to regenerate changed components. Export updates your platform context files (CLAUDE.md, AGENTS.md, .cursorrules) automatically. Skill generation becomes routine — like running tests.
```

  "new_string": "The brief's `doc_urls` field drives the doc_fetcher step. The agent uses whatever web fetching tool is available in its environment (Firecrawl, WebFetch, curl, etc.) to retrieve documentation as markdown and extract API information with T3 citations.\n\n### Scenario E: Rename a Skill\n\nYou generated a quick skill for `cognee` and now want a more specific name to distinguish it from the official one.\n\n```\n@Ferris RS\n# Ferris asks: Which skill? → cognee\n# Ferris asks: New name? → cognee-skf-community\n# Ferris copies to new name across all versions, verifies every reference,\n# updates the export manifest, rebuilds CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md,\n# then deletes the old name.\n```\n\nTransactional safety: if verification fails, the old skill stays intact.\n\n### Scenario F: Drop a Deprecated Version\n\nYou have `cognee` with versions 0.1.0, 0.5.0, and 0.6.0 (active). Version 0.1.0 is obsolete.\n\n```\n@Ferris DS\n# Ferris asks: Which skill? → cognee\n# Ferris asks: Which version? → 0.1.0\n# Ferris asks: Deprecate (keep files) or Purge (delete)? → Purge\n# Ferris updates the manifest, rebuilds context files, deletes the 0.1.0 directory.\n```\n\nVersion 0.6.0 remains active. Version 0.5.0 is untouched. The managed sections in CLAUDE.md/AGENTS.md no longer reference 0.1.0.\n\n---\n\n## Tips & Tricks"
}

Excellent! Now I have a complete picture. Let me create a comprehensive summary document.

## Summary: SKF Installer Detection and Update Logic

Based on my analysis of the installer code, here's exactly how the SKF installer detects existing installations and handles version management:

### 1. **Detection Logic** (`tools/cli/lib/ui.js`, lines 71-82)

The detection happens in the `detectInstallation()` method:

```javascript
async detectInstallation(projectDir) {
  const hasBmadSkf = await fs.pathExists(path.join(projectDir, SKF_FOLDER));
  const hasBmadDir = await fs.pathExists(path.join(projectDir, '_bmad'));

  if (hasBmadSkf) {
    return { type: 'existing', folder: SKF_FOLDER };
  }
  if (hasBmadDir) {
    return { type: 'bmad-ready', folder: SKF_FOLDER };
  }
  return { type: 'fresh', folder: SKF_FOLDER };
}
```

**Key point:** It simply checks if `_bmad/skf/` folder exists. No version comparison happens here yet.

---

### 2. **The Prompt Code** (`tools/cli/lib/ui.js`, lines 96-112)

When an existing installation is detected, the user sees this prompt:

```javascript
if (detection.type === 'existing') {
  log.warn(`Found existing installation at ${chalk.white(SKF_FOLDER + '/')}`);

  const choice = await select({
    message: 'What would you like to do?',
    options: [
      { label: 'Update — Replace SKF files, keep config.yaml', value: 'update' },
      { label: 'Fresh install — Remove everything and start over', value: 'fresh' },
      { label: 'Cancel', value: 'cancel' },
    ],
  });

  if (isCancel(choice) || choice === 'cancel') {
    cancel('Installation cancelled.');
    return { cancelled: true };
  }
  action = choice;
}
```

**Note:** This prompt does NOT currently show version information or ask about skipping if versions match.

---

### 3. **Version Storage & Reading**

#### **New Version (from installer):**
- **Location:** `package.json` at the repository root  
- **Field:** `"version": "0.8.4"` (currently at line 4)
- **Read by:** `tools/cli/lib/installer.js`, line 146:
  ```javascript
  const packageJson = require('../../../package.json');
  await writeManifest(projectDir, config, {
    version: packageJson.version,
    ideFiles,
  });
  ```
- Also written to `_bmad/skf/VERSION` file (line 191 of installer.js):
  ```javascript
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(skfDir, 'VERSION'), packageJson.version, 'utf8');
  ```

#### **Currently Installed Version:**
- **Location:** `_bmad/_config/skf-manifest.yaml`
- **Field:** `version:` (line 67 in manifest.js)
- **Read by:** `tools/cli/lib/manifest.js`, `readManifest()` function (lines 99-107):
  ```javascript
  async function readManifest(projectDir) {
    const manifestPath = path.join(projectDir, MANIFEST_DIR, MANIFEST_FILE);
    try {
      const content = await fs.readFile(manifestPath, 'utf8');
      return yaml.load(content) || null;
    } catch {
      return null;
    }
  }
  ```
- Alternative fallback: `_bmad/skf/VERSION` file contains just the version string

---

### 4. **Version Comparison Logic**

**Current state:** Version comparison EXISTS but is NOT used during install prompt.

The comparison function is in `tools/cli/lib/version-check.js`, lines 45-54:

```javascript
function compareVersions(current, latest) {
  const parse = (v) => v.replace(/^v/, '').split('.').map(Number);
  const [cMajor, cMinor, cPatch] = parse(current);
  const [lMajor, lMinor, lPatch] = parse(latest);

  if (lMajor > cMajor) return true;
  if (lMajor === cMajor && lMinor > cMinor) return true;
  if (lMajor === cMajor && lMinor === cMinor && lPatch > cPatch) return true;
  return false;
}
```

This function:
- Parses semver strings (e.g., "0.8.4")
- Returns `true` if `latest` > `current`
- Returns `false` if versions are equal or current is newer

**Usage:** Currently used only for checking npm registry for updates (version-check.js, line 60-72) — a non-blocking notification printed after commands finish.

---

### 5. **Manifest File Structure**

**Path:** `_bmad/_config/skf-manifest.yaml`

**Example content (from manifest.js):**
```yaml
version: 0.8.4
installed_at: 2026-04-04T12:34:56.789Z
action: fresh
module: skf
skf_folder: _bmad/skf
ides:

  - cursor

```

---

### 6. **Update vs. Fresh Install Flow**

**Update action** (tools/cli/lib/installer.js, lines 28-45):
- Reads existing `config.yaml` and saves it to `config._savedConfigYaml`
- Deletes the entire `_bmad/skf/` directory
- Re-installs files
- Restores the saved `config.yaml` (preserving user settings)

**Fresh install action** (lines 50-53):
- Also deletes `_bmad/skf/` if it exists
- Re-installs from scratch
- Prompts user for all settings again (with pre-population from saved config)

---

### 7. **Version Comparison NOT Currently Implemented in Install Flow**

**Gap:** The installer does NOT:
- ✗ Compare installed version (from manifest) with new version (from package.json) during the prompt
- ✗ Skip the prompt if versions are the same
- ✗ Show version information in the "Found existing installation" message
- ✗ Auto-suggest "Update" vs "Fresh" based on version difference

**To add this, you would need to:**

1. In `ui.js` `promptInstall()`, after calling `detectInstallation()`:
   ```javascript
   const oldManifest = detection.type === 'existing' 
     ? await readManifest(projectDir) 
     : null;
   const newVersion = require('../../../package.json').version;
   const oldVersion = oldManifest?.version;
   ```

2. Modify the warning message to show versions:
   ```javascript
   log.warn(
     `Found existing installation at ${chalk.white(SKF_FOLDER + '/')}` +
     (oldVersion ? ` (v${oldVersion}, new: v${newVersion})` : '')
   );
   ```

3. Import and use `compareVersions`:
   ```javascript
   const { compareVersions } = require('./version-check');
   const isNewer = oldVersion && compareVersions(oldVersion, newVersion);
   ```

---

### **Key Files to Modify**

If you want to add version-aware prompting:

1. **`/home/armel/Projects/OSS/bmad-module-skill-forge/tools/cli/lib/ui.js`** — Add version display and skip logic
2. **`/home/armel/Projects/OSS/bmad-module-skill-forge/tools/cli/lib/manifest.js`** — Already handles reading/writing version
3. **`/home/armel/Projects/OSS/bmad-module-skill-forge/tools/cli/lib/version-check.js`** — Already has `compareVersions()` function

---

| **Manifest Write** | `installer.js` line 147 | Writes manifest after install |
| **Manifest Read** | `manifest.js` line 99 | `readManifest()` function |

96	/**
97	 * Read an existing manifest, or return null if none exists.
98	 */
99	async function readManifest(projectDir) {
100	  const manifestPath = path.join(projectDir, MANIFEST_DIR, MANIFEST_FILE);
101	  try {
102	    const content = await fs.readFile(manifestPath, 'utf8');
103	    return yaml.load(content) || null;
104	  } catch {
105	    return null;
106	  }
107	}
108

45	function compareVersions(current, latest) {
46	  const parse = (v) => v.replace(/^v/, '').split('.').map(Number);
47	  const [cMajor, cMinor, cPatch] = parse(current);
48	  const [lMajor, lMinor, lPatch] = parse(latest);
49	
50	  if (lMajor > cMajor) return true;
51	  if (lMajor === cMajor && lMinor > cMinor) return true;
52	  if (lMajor === cMajor && lMinor === cMinor && lPatch > cPatch) return true;
53	  return false;
54	}
55	
56	/**
57	 * Start an async version check. Call the returned function after your
58	 * command finishes to print the update notice (if any).
59	 */
60	function startVersionCheck(currentVersion) {
61	  const checkPromise = fetchLatestVersion().then((latestVersion) => {
62	    if (!latestVersion || !compareVersions(currentVersion, latestVersion)) {
63	      return null;
64	    }
65	    return (
66	      '\n' +
67	      chalk.hex('#F59E0B')(`  Update available: ${chalk.dim(currentVersion)} → ${chalk.hex('#FBBF24').bold(latestVersion)}`) +
68	      '\n' +
69	      chalk.dim(`  Run: npx bmad-module-skill-forge@latest install`) +

  "file_path": "/home/armel/.claude/plans/reflective-skipping-wolf.md",
  "content": "# Plan: Show Installed and Incoming Versions in the Installer Prompt\n\n## Context\n\nWhen a user runs `npx bmad-module-skill-forge install` on an existing installation, the installer detects it and shows the Update/Fresh install/Cancel prompt — but it does NOT show which version is currently installed or which version is about to be installed. Users don't know if they are actually upgrading, reinstalling the same version, or downgrading.\n\nThe fix is small and fully additive: read the installed version from the manifest, read the incoming version from `package.json`, and display both in the \"Found existing installation\" message. Adjust the option labels to reflect the version delta (upgrade vs same-version reinstall).\n\n**All required utilities already exist:**\n- `readManifest(projectDir)` — `tools/cli/lib/manifest.js:99` — reads `_bmad/_config/skf-manifest.yaml`, returns `{ version, ... }` or `null`\n- `compareVersions(current, latest)` — `tools/cli/lib/version-check.js:45` — returns `true` if `latest > current` (already exported at line 86)\n- `package.json` version is already read in `ui.js:31` for the banner\n\n---\n\n## Design\n\nThe only file that changes is `tools/cli/lib/ui.js`. The change is localized to the `promptInstall()` method, in the block that handles `detection.type === 'existing'`.\n\n### New message format\n\n**Case 1 — Upgrade (installed < incoming):**\n```\nFound existing installation at _bmad/skf/ (v0.7.2 → v0.8.4 available)\n```\n\n**Case 2 — Same version (installed === incoming):**\n```\nFound existing installation at _bmad/skf/ (v0.8.4 — already at this version)\n```\n\n**Case 3 — Downgrade (installed > incoming):**\n```\nFound existing installation at _bmad/skf/ (v0.9.0 → v0.8.4, DOWNGRADE)\n```\n\n**Case 4 — Manifest missing or lacks version (legacy install):**\n```\nFound existing installation at _bmad/skf/ (version unknown → v0.8.4)\n```\n\n### Prompt option labels\n\nSlightly adjust the option labels so they're self-explanatory given the version delta:\n\n- **Upgrade case:** `Update — Upgrade from v0.7.2 to v0.8.4, keep config.yaml`\n- **Same version:** `Update — Reinstall v0.8.4, keep config.yaml`\n- **Downgrade:** `Update — Downgrade to v0.8.4, keep config.yaml` (user accepts the risk)\n- **Unknown:** `Update — Install v0.8.4, keep config.yaml`\n\nThe Fresh install and Cancel options remain unchanged.\n\n### Why not block same-version reinstalls\n\nSome users re-run the installer intentionally: to repair a corrupted install, to re-index QMD collections, or to regenerate IDE command files. Blocking would be annoying. The prompt simply makes it clear what's happening.\n\n---\n\n## Files to Modify\n\n### `tools/cli/lib/ui.js`\n\n**1. Add imports at the top of the file** (after line 17):\n```javascript\nconst { readManifest } = require('./manifest');\nconst { compareVersions } = require('./version-check');\n```\n\n**2. Modify the `detection.type === 'existing'` block** (lines 96-112). Replace:\n```javascript\nif (detection.type === 'existing') {\n  log.warn(`Found existing installation at ${chalk.white(SKF_FOLDER + '/')}`);\n\n  const choice = await select({\n    message: 'What would you like to do?',\n    options: [\n      { label: 'Update — Replace SKF files, keep config.yaml', value: 'update' },\n      { label: 'Fresh install — Remove everything and start over', value: 'fresh' },\n      { label: 'Cancel', value: 'cancel' },\n    ],\n  });\n  ...\n}\n```\n\nWith a version-aware version that:\n1. Calls `readManifest(projectDir)` to get the installed version (tolerates missing manifest → `null`)\n2. Reads `package.json.version` (already imported at line 31 for the banner — reuse the variable)\n3. Computes the version delta: `upgrade`, `same`, `downgrade`, or `unknown`\n4. Builds a version-aware warning string\n5. Builds a version-aware Update label string\n6. Passes both to the existing `log.warn` and `select` calls\n\nPseudocode of the inserted logic:\n```javascript\nconst existingManifest = await readManifest(projectDir);\nconst installedVersion = existingManifest?.version || null;\nconst incomingVersion = require('../../../package.json').version;\n\nlet versionLabel;\nlet updateOptionLabel;\nif (!installedVersion) {\n  versionLabel = `version unknown → v${incomingVersion}`;\n  updateOptionLabel = `Update — Install v${incomingVersion}, keep config.yaml`;\n} else if (installedVersion === incomingVersion) {\n  versionLabel = `v${installedVersion} — already at this version`;\n  updateOptionLabel = `Update — Reinstall v${incomingVersion}, keep config.yaml`;\n} else if (compareVersions(installedVersion, incomingVersion)) {\n  versionLabel = `v${installedVersion} → v${incomingVersion} available`;\n  updateOptionLabel = `Update — Upgrade from v${installedVersion} to v${incomingVersion}, keep config.yaml`;\n} else {\n  versionLabel = `v${installedVersion} → v${incomingVersion}, DOWNGRADE`;\n  updateOptionLabel = `Update — Downgrade to v${incomingVersion}, keep config.yaml`;\n}\n\nlog.warn(`Found existing installation at ${chalk.white(SKF_FOLDER + '/')} (${versionLabel})`);\n\nconst choice = await select({\n  message: 'What would you like to do?',\n  options: [\n    { label: updateOptionLabel, value: 'update' },\n    { label: 'Fresh install — Remove everything and start over', value: 'fresh' },\n    { label: 'Cancel', value: 'cancel' },\n  ],\n});\n```\n\nThe rest of the block (cancel handling, `action = choice`) stays unchanged.\n\n---\n\n## Files NOT Modified\n\n- `tools/cli/lib/manifest.js` — `readManifest` already does what we need\n- `tools/cli/lib/version-check.js` — `compareVersions` already exported\n- `tools/cli/lib/installer.js` — no changes needed; update flow unchanged\n- `package.json` — version field already exists\n- `test/test-cli-integration.js` — existing Test Suite 2 (\"Update Preserves Config\") already validates the update flow without asserting on label text. No test changes needed unless we want to explicitly assert on the new message (optional follow-up).\n\n---\n\n## Verification\n\n1. **Fresh install flow:** Run installer in a directory without `_bmad/skf/`. Verify behavior unchanged — banner, prompt for new install, no version comparison shown.\n2. **Same-version update:** Install v0.8.4, then re-run the installer. Verify message shows `(v0.8.4 — already at this version)` and update label says `Reinstall v0.8.4`.\n3. **Upgrade simulation:** Manually edit `_bmad/_config/skf-manifest.yaml` to set `version: 0.7.0`. Re-run installer. Verify message shows `(v0.7.0 → v0.8.4 available)` and update label says `Upgrade from v0.7.0 to v0.8.4`.\n4. **Downgrade simulation:** Manually edit manifest to set `version: 0.9.9`. Re-run installer. Verify message shows `(v0.9.9 → v0.8.4, DOWNGRADE)`.\n5. **Missing manifest:** Delete `_bmad/_config/skf-manifest.yaml` but keep `_bmad/skf/` directory. Re-run installer. Verify message shows `(version unknown → v0.8.4)` and installer still offers Update/Fresh/Cancel.\n6. **Existing tests:** Run `npm test` — all 376+ tests should continue to pass. The installer integration test (Test Suite 2 in test-cli-integration.js) verifies update flow but does not assert on label text, so no test updates required.\n"
}

## Approved Plan:
# Plan: Show Installed and Incoming Versions in the Installer Prompt

## Context

When a user runs `npx bmad-module-skill-forge install` on an existing installation, the installer detects it and shows the Update/Fresh install/Cancel prompt — but it does NOT show which version is currently installed or which version is about to be installed. Users don't know if they are actually upgrading, reinstalling the same version, or downgrading.

The fix is small and fully additive: read the installed version from the manifest, read the incoming version from `package.json`, and display both in the "Found existing installation" message. Adjust the option labels to reflect the version delta (upgrade vs same-version reinstall).

**All required utilities already exist:**
- `readManifest(projectDir)` — `tools/cli/lib/manifest.js:99` — reads `_bmad/_config/skf-manifest.yaml`, returns `{ version, ... }` or `null`
- `compareVersions(current, latest)` — `tools/cli/lib/version-check.js:45` — returns `true` if `latest > current` (already exported at line 86)
- `package.json` version is already read in `ui.js:31` for the banner

---

## Design

The only file that changes is `tools/cli/lib/ui.js`. The change is localized to the `promptInstall()` method, in the block that handles `detection.type === 'existing'`.

### New message format

**Case 1 — Upgrade (installed < incoming):**
```
Found existing installation at _bmad/skf/ (v0.7.2 → v0.8.4 available)
```

**Case 2 — Same version (installed === incoming):**
```
Found existing installation at _bmad/skf/ (v0.8.4 — already at this version)
```

**Case 3 — Downgrade (installed > incoming):**
```
Found existing installation at _bmad/skf/ (v0.9.0 → v0.8.4, DOWNGRADE)
```

**Case 4 — Manifest missing or lacks version (legacy install):**
```
Found existing installation at _bmad/skf/ (version unknown → v0.8.4)
```

### Prompt option labels

Slightly adjust the option labels so they're self-explanatory given the version delta:

- **Upgrade case:** `Update — Upgrade from v0.7.2 to v0.8.4, keep config.yaml`
- **Same version:** `Update — Reinstall v0.8.4, keep config.yaml`
- **Downgrade:** `Update — Downgrade to v0.8.4, keep config.yaml` (user accepts the risk)
- **Unknown:** `Update — Install v0.8.4, keep config.yaml`

The Fresh install and Cancel options remain unchanged.

### Why not block same-version reinstalls

Some users re-run the installer intentionally: to repair a corrupted install, to re-index QMD collections, or to regenerate IDE command files. Blocking would be annoying. The prompt simply makes it clear what's happening.

---

## Files to Modify

### `tools/cli/lib/ui.js`

**1. Add imports at the top of the file** (after line 17):
```javascript
const { readManifest } = require('./manifest');
const { compareVersions } = require('./version-check');
```

**2. Modify the `detection.type === 'existing'` block** (lines 96-112). Replace:
```javascript
if (detection.type === 'existing') {
  log.warn(`Found existing installation at ${chalk.white(SKF_FOLDER + '/')}`);

  const choice = await select({
    message: 'What would you like to do?',
    options: [
      { label: 'Update — Replace SKF files, keep config.yaml', value: 'update' },
      { label: 'Fresh install — Remove everything and start over', value: 'fresh' },
      { label: 'Cancel', value: 'cancel' },
    ],
  });
  ...
}
```

With a version-aware version that:
1. Calls `readManifest(projectDir)` to get the installed version (tolerates missing manifest → `null`)
2. Reads `package.json.version` (already imported at line 31 for the banner — reuse the variable)
3. Computes the version delta: `upgrade`, `same`, `downgrade`, or `unknown`
4. Builds a version-aware warning string
5. Builds a version-aware Update label string
6. Passes both to the existing `log.warn` and `select` calls

Pseudocode of the inserted logic:
```javascript
const existingManifest = await readManifest(projectDir);
const installedVersion = existingManifest?.version || null;
const incomingVersion = require('../../../package.json').version;

let versionLabel;
let updateOptionLabel;
if (!installedVersion) {
  versionLabel = `version unknown → v${incomingVersion}`;
  updateOptionLabel = `Update — Install v${incomingVersion}, keep config.yaml`;
} else if (installedVersion === incomingVersion) {
  versionLabel = `v${installedVersion} — already at this version`;
  updateOptionLabel = `Update — Reinstall v${incomingVersion}, keep config.yaml`;
} else if (compareVersions(installedVersion, incomingVersion)) {
  versionLabel = `v${installedVersion} → v${incomingVersion} available`;
  updateOptionLabel = `Update — Upgrade from v${installedVersion} to v${incomingVersion}, keep config.yaml`;
} else {
  versionLabel = `v${installedVersion} → v${incomingVersion}, DOWNGRADE`;
  updateOptionLabel = `Update — Downgrade to v${incomingVersion}, keep config.yaml`;
}

log.warn(`Found existing installation at ${chalk.white(SKF_FOLDER + '/')} (${versionLabel})`);

const choice = await select({
  message: 'What would you like to do?',
  options: [
    { label: updateOptionLabel, value: 'update' },
    { label: 'Fresh install — Remove everything and start over', value: 'fresh' },
    { label: 'Cancel', value: 'cancel' },
  ],
});
```

The rest of the block (cancel handling, `action = choice`) stays unchanged.

---

## Files NOT Modified

- `tools/cli/lib/manifest.js` — `readManifest` already does what we need
- `tools/cli/lib/version-check.js` — `compareVersions` already exported
- `tools/cli/lib/installer.js` — no changes needed; update flow unchanged
- `package.json` — version field already exists
- `test/test-cli-integration.js` — existing Test Suite 2 ("Update Preserves Config") already validates the update flow without asserting on label text. No test changes needed unless we want to explicitly assert on the new message (optional follow-up).

---

## Verification

1. **Fresh install flow:** Run installer in a directory without `_bmad/skf/`. Verify behavior unchanged — banner, prompt for new install, no version comparison shown.
2. **Same-version update:** Install v0.8.4, then re-run the installer. Verify message shows `(v0.8.4 — already at this version)` and update label says `Reinstall v0.8.4`.
3. **Upgrade simulation:** Manually edit `_bmad/_config/skf-manifest.yaml` to set `version: 0.7.0`. Re-run installer. Verify message shows `(v0.7.0 → v0.8.4 available)` and update label says `Upgrade from v0.7.0 to v0.8.4`.
4. **Downgrade simulation:** Manually edit manifest to set `version: 0.9.9`. Re-run installer. Verify message shows `(v0.9.9 → v0.8.4, DOWNGRADE)`.
5. **Missing manifest:** Delete `_bmad/_config/skf-manifest.yaml` but keep `_bmad/skf/` directory. Re-run installer. Verify message shows `(version unknown → v0.8.4)` and installer still offers Update/Fresh/Cancel.
6. **Existing tests:** Run `npm test` — all 376+ tests should continue to pass. The installer integration test (Test Suite 2 in test-cli-integration.js) verifies update flow but does not assert on label text, so no test updates required.

```

> AGENT

Plan approved. This is a small, localized change — implementing directly.
